    };
  }
  /**
   * Returns the RGBA values interpolated into a string with the following format:
   * "RGBA(xxx, xxx, xxx, xx)".
   */
  toRgbString() {
    const t = Math.round(this.r), n = Math.round(this.g), l = Math.round(this.b);
    return this.a === 1 ? `rgb(${t}, ${n}, ${l})` : `rgba(${t}, ${n}, ${l}, ${this.roundA})`;
  }
  /**
   * Returns the object as a RGBA object.
   */
  toPercentageRgb() {
    const t = (n) => `${Math.round(ut(n, 255) * 100)}%`;
    return {
      r: t(this.r),
      g: t(this.g),
      b: t(this.b),
      a: this.a
    };
  }
  /**
   * Returns the RGBA relative values interpolated into a string
   */
  toPercentageRgbString() {
    const t = (n) => Math.round(ut(n, 255) * 100);
    return this.a === 1 ? `rgb(${t(this.r)}%, ${t(this.g)}%, ${t(this.b)}%)` : `rgba(${t(this.r)}%, ${t(this.g)}%, ${t(this.b)}%, ${this.roundA})`;
  }
  toCmyk() {
    return {
      ...To(this.r, this.g, this.b)
    };
  }
  toCmykString() {
    const { c: t, m: n, y: l, k: i } = To(this.r, this.g, this.b);
    return `cmyk(${t}, ${n}, ${l}, ${i})`;
  }
  /**
   * The 'real' name of the color -if there is one.
   */
  toName() {
    if (this.a === 0)
      return "transparent";
    if (this.a < 1)
      return !1;
    const t = "#" + Eo(this.r, this.g, this.b, !1);
    for (const [n, l] of Object.entries(Ba))
      if (t === l)
        return n;
    return !1;
  }
  toString(t) {
    const n = !!t;
    t = t ?? this.format;
    let l = !1;
    const i = this.a < 1 && this.a >= 0;
    return !n && i && (t.startsWith("hex") || t === "name") ? t === "name" && this.a === 0 ? this.toName() : this.toRgbString() : (t === "rgb" && (l = this.toRgbString()), t === "prgb" && (l = this.toPercentageRgbString()), (t === "hex" || t === "hex6") && (l = this.toHexString()), t === "hex3" && (l = this.toHexString(!0)), t === "hex4" && (l = this.toHex8String(!0)), t === "hex8" && (l = this.toHex8String()), t === "name" && (l = this.toName()), t === "hsl" && (l = this.toHslString()), t === "hsv" && (l = this.toHsvString()), t === "cmyk" && (l = this.toCmykString()), l || this.toHexString());
  }
  toNumber() {
    return (Math.round(this.r) << 16) + (Math.round(this.g) << 8) + Math.round(this.b);
  }
  clone() {
    return new Ge(this.toString());
  }
  /**
   * Lighten the color a given amount. Providing 100 will always return white.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  lighten(t = 10) {
    const n = this.toHsl();
    return n.l += t / 100, n.l = Xl(n.l), new Ge(n);
  }
  /**
   * Brighten the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  brighten(t = 10) {
    const n = this.toRgb();
    return n.r = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, n.r - Math.round(255 * -(t / 100)))), n.g = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, n.g - Math.round(255 * -(t / 100)))), n.b = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, n.b - Math.round(255 * -(t / 100)))), new Ge(n);
  }
  /**
   * Darken the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 100 will always return black.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  darken(t = 10) {
    const n = this.toHsl();
    return n.l -= t / 100, n.l = Xl(n.l), new Ge(n);
  }
  /**
   * Mix the color with pure white, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 0 will do nothing, providing 100 will always return white.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  tint(t = 10) {
    return this.mix("white", t);
  }
  /**
   * Mix the color with pure black, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 0 will do nothing, providing 100 will always return black.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  shade(t = 10) {
    return this.mix("black", t);
  }
  /**
   * Desaturate the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 100 will is the same as calling greyscale
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  desaturate(t = 10) {
    const n = this.toHsl();
    return n.s -= t / 100, n.s = Xl(n.s), new Ge(n);
  }
  /**
   * Saturate the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  saturate(t = 10) {
    const n = this.toHsl();
    return n.s += t / 100, n.s = Xl(n.s), new Ge(n);
  }
  /**
   * Completely desaturates a color into greyscale.
   * Same as calling `desaturate(100)`
   */
  greyscale() {
    return this.desaturate(100);
  }
  /**
   * Spin takes a positive or negative amount within [-360, 360] indicating the change of hue.
   * Values outside of this range will be wrapped into this range.
   */
  spin(t) {
    const n = this.toHsl(), l = (n.h + t) % 360;
    return n.h = l < 0 ? 360 + l : l, new Ge(n);
  }
  /**
   * Mix the current color a given amount with another color, from 0 to 100.
   * 0 means no mixing (return current color).
   */
  mix(t, n = 50) {
    const l = this.toRgb(), i = new Ge(t).toRgb(), s = n / 100, o = {
      r: (i.r - l.r) * s + l.r,
      g: (i.g - l.g) * s + l.g,
      b: (i.b - l.b) * s + l.b,
      a: (i.a - l.a) * s + l.a
    };
    return new Ge(o);
  }
  analogous(t = 6, n = 30) {
    const l = this.toHsl(), i = 360 / n, s = [this];
    for (l.h = (l.h - (i * t >> 1) + 720) % 360; --t; )
      l.h = (l.h + i) % 360, s.push(new Ge(l));
    return s;
  }
  /**
   * taken from https://github.com/infusion/jQuery-xcolor/blob/master/jquery.xcolor.js
   */
  complement() {
    const t = this.toHsl();
    return t.h = (t.h + 180) % 360, new Ge(t);
  }
  monochromatic(t = 6) {
    const n = this.toHsv(), { h: l } = n, { s: i } = n;
    let { v: s } = n;
    const o = [], r = 1 / t;
    for (; t--; )
      o.push(new Ge({ h: l, s: i, v: s })), s = (s + r) % 1;
    return o;
  }
  splitcomplement() {
    const t = this.toHsl(), { h: n } = t;
    return [
      this,
      new Ge({ h: (n + 72) % 360, s: t.s, l: t.l }),
      new Ge({ h: (n + 216) % 360, s: t.s, l: t.l })
    ];
  }
  /**
   * Compute how the color would appear on a background
   */
  onBackground(t) {
    const n = this.toRgb(), l = new Ge(t).toRgb(), i = n.a + l.a * (1 - n.a);
    return new Ge({
      r: (n.r * n.a + l.r * l.a * (1 - n.a)) / i,
      g: (n.g * n.a + l.g * l.a * (1 - n.a)) / i,
      b: (n.b * n.a + l.b * l.a * (1 - n.a)) / i,
      a: i
    });
  }
  /**
   * Alias for `polyad(3)`
   */
  triad() {
    return this.polyad(3);
  }
  /**
   * Alias for `polyad(4)`
   */
  tetrad() {
    return this.polyad(4);
  }
  /**
   * Get polyad colors, like (for 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, etc...)
   * monad, dyad, triad, tetrad, pentad, hexad, heptad, octad, etc...
   */
  polyad(t) {
    const n = this.toHsl(), { h: l } = n, i = [this], s = 360 / t;
    for (let o = 1; o < t; o++)
      i.push(new Ge({ h: (l + o * s) % 360, s: n.s, l: n.l }));
    return i;
  }
  /**
   * compare color vs current color
   */
  equals(t) {
    const n = new Ge(t);
    return this.format === "cmyk" || n.format === "cmyk" ? this.toCmykString() === n.toCmykString() : this.toRgbString() === n.toRgbString();
  }
}
function Kt(e, t = 20) {
  return e.mix("#141414", t).toString();
}

function cp(e, t) {
  const n = e.width * e.tileSize, l = e.height * e.tileSize, i = Math.min(t.w / n, t.h / l), s = n * i, o = l * i, r = t.x + (t.w - s) / 2, c = t.y + (t.h - o) / 2;
  return Object.freeze({
    scale: i,
    rect: Object.freeze({ x: r, y: c, w: s, h: o }),
    worldToDisplay(d, u) {
      return [r + d * i, c + u * i];
    },
    displayToWorld(d, u) {
      return d < r || u < c || d >= r + s || u >= c + o ? null : [(d - r) / i, (u - c) / i];
    },
    tileCenterToDisplay(d, u, k = 0, A = 0) {
      return this.worldToDisplay((d - k) * 16 + 8, (u - A) * 16 + 8);
    }
  });
}

      q = "此原操作已取消，未產生副本。", we();
      return;
    }
    if (w.state === "failed") {
      q = k + "失敗：" + Fe(w.failedCode) + "。這次的資料已由服務端丟棄（名稱已釋放），可修改後直接重新複製。", R(), we();
      return;
    }
    if (!xe()) {
      q = "此原操作已結束，未產生副本。", we();
      return;
    }
    if (h = !1, await J(S, B), !(!Z(S) || g !== B || !w?.draftRevision))
      return R(), q = u ? "完整副本已建立；遊戲清單已自動更新，可在「遊戲管理」直接載入。" : "新遊戲已建立，可在下方「我的遊戲」中編輯。", u ? o() : await ht(S), u ? "複製完成。" : "新遊戲已建立。";
  }
  const Q = 2e3, G = 900 * 1e3, Y = 3;
  async function J(S, B) {
    const te = Date.now();
    let ee = 0, fe = null, be = null;
    const re = () => k + "仍在處理（" + (w?.state ?? "pending") + "）；" + ve() + "…", Oe = () => {

    }, Me = () => {
      ee++, fe = e(P(B) + "/run", {}, { revision: w.rowRevision }).then((je) => {
        be = { data: je };
      }, (je) => {
        be = { error: je };
      });

        return;
      }
      if (w?.state === "failed") {
        q = k + "失敗：" + Fe(w.failedCode) + "。這次的資料已由服務端丟棄（名稱已釋放），可修改後直接重新複製。", R(), we();
        return;
      }
      if (Date.now() - te > G) {
        Oe();
        return;
      }
      const je = w?.state === "running" && Number(w.leaseUntil ?? 0) > Date.now();
      if (!fe && !je) {
        if (ee >= Y) {
          Oe();
          return;
        }
        Me(), h = !1;
      }
      if (q = re(), we(), await new Promise((Qe) => setTimeout(Qe, Q)), !Z(S) || g !== B) return;
      if (be) {
        const Qe = be;
        if (be = null, fe = null, Qe.error) {
          if (He(Qe.error)) {
            ze("服務端已無此原操作；本機追蹤已清除，可直接重新建立。");
            return;
          }
          q = "回應中斷，正在核對同一個請求的實際結果…", we();
        } else w = tt(Qe.data, w.gameId);
        continue;
      }
      let Se = null, ot = 0;
      for (; ; )
        try {
          Se = await e(P(B));
          break;
        } catch (Qe) {
          if (!Z(S) || g !== B) throw Qe;
          if (He(Qe)) {
            ze("服務端已無此原操作；本機追蹤已清除，可直接重新建立。");
            return;
          }
          if (++ot >= 3) throw Qe;
          if (await new Promise((j) => setTimeout(j, Q)), !Z(S) || g !== B) return;
        }
      if (!Z(S) || g !== B) return;
      w = tt(Se, w.gameId), h = !1;
    }
  }
  const le = (S) => (...B) => n(async () => {
    if (!oe()) return;
    const te = K();
    try {
      return await S(...B);
    } catch (ee) {
      throw Z(te) && (q = "操作未確認：" + ee.message), ee;
    } finally {
      we();
    }
  });
  function W(S = !1) {
    return !(v || T || Re()) || !S && c((u ? "複製遊戲" : "新建輸入") + "或原操作尚未完成。離開不會自動保存或取消服務操作；仍要離開？");
  }
  const p = Object.freeze({
    refresh: le(async () => {
      const S = K();
      await nt(S), Z(S) && !u && await ht(S);
    }),
    create: le(O),
    // 重新載入後接著跑完手上那一筆原鍵（不建立第二份）。
    resume: le(async () => {
      g && await H(K());
    }),
    // 失敗彈窗的「確認」：只關掉這筆結果，不改服務端任何事。
    dismiss() {
      !oe() || l() || (w = null, q = "", we());
    },
    forget: le(async () => {
      try {
        r.removeItem(ie());
      } catch {
        $ = !0;
      }
      g = null, w = null, h = !1, q = "已清除本機追蹤；未取消或回滾任何在途請求，服務端結果仍以查詢同一個請求為準。";
    }),
    setField(S, B) {
      !oe() || l() || !["name", "introduction"].includes(S) || typeof B != "string" || (S === "name" ? v = B : T = B, we());
    },
    gotoGame: le(async (S) => {
      const B = K();
      C.some((te) => te.gameId === S) && W() && Z(B) && await s(S);
    })
  });
  return Object.freeze({
    actions: p,
    render: we,
    canLeave: W,
    setIdentity(S) {
      if (I === S) return we();
      M++, I = S, w = null, C = [], v = "", T = "", q = "", h = !1, Be(), we(), g && oe() && p.resume();
    },
    pagehide() {
      M++, w = null, C = [], h = !!g, we();
    }
  });
}
